### [74] [Beyond Match Maximization and Fairness: Retention-Optimized Two-Sided Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15752)
*Ren Kishimoto,Rikiya Takehi,Koichi Tanaka,Masahiro Nomura,Riku Togashi,Yoji Tomita,Yuta Saito*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: On two-sided matching platforms such as online dating and recruiting, recommendation algorithms often aim to maximize the total number of matches. However, this objective creates an imbalance, where some users receive far too many matches while many others receive very few and eventually abandon the platform. Retaining users is crucial for many platforms, such as those that depend heavily on subscriptions. Some may use fairness objectives to solve the problem of match maximization. However, fairness in itself is not the ultimate objective for many platforms, as users do not suddenly reward the platform simply because exposure is equalized. In practice, where user retention is often the ultimate goal, casually relying on fairness will leave the optimization of retention up to luck.
  In this work, instead of maximizing matches or axiomatically defining fairness, we formally define the new problem setting of maximizing user retention in two-sided matching platforms. To this end, we introduce a dynamic learning-to-rank (LTR) algorithm called Matching for Retention (MRet). Unlike conventional algorithms for two-sided matching, our approach models user retention by learning personalized retention curves from each user's profile and interaction history. Based on these curves, MRet dynamically adapts recommendations by jointly considering the retention gains of both the user receiving recommendations and those who are being recommended, so that limited matching opportunities can be allocated where they most improve overall retention. Naturally but importantly, empirical evaluations on synthetic and real-world datasets from a major online dating platform show that MRet achieves higher user retention, since conventional methods optimize matches or fairness rather than retention.

Shen,Yuhao Yang,Yujiang Li,Yunan Liu,Yunqing Wang,Yuntao Li,Yurong Wu,Yutao Zhang,Yuxi Duan,Yuxuan Zhang,Zezhen Liu,Zhengtao Jiang,Zhenhe Yan,Zheyu Zhang,Zhixiang Wei,Zhuo Chen,Zhuoer Feng,Zijun Yao,Ziwei Chai,Ziyuan Wang,Zuzhou Zhang,Bin Xu,Minlie Huang,Hongning Wang,Juanzi Li,Yuxiao Dong,Jie Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: We present GLM-5, a next-generation foundation model designed to transition the paradigm of vibe coding to agentic engineering. Building upon the agentic, reasoning, and coding (ARC) capabilities of its predecessor, GLM-5 adopts DSA to significantly reduce training and inference costs while maintaining long-context fidelity. To advance model alignment and autonomy, we implement a new asynchronous reinforcement learning infrastructure that drastically improves post-training efficiency by decoupling generation from training. Furthermore, we propose novel asynchronous agent RL algorithms that further improve RL quality, enabling the model to learn from complex, long-horizon interactions more effectively. Through these innovations, GLM-5 achieves state-of-the-art performance on major open benchmarks. Most critically, GLM-5 demonstrates unprecedented capability in real-world coding tasks, surpassing previous baselines in handling end-to-end software engineering challenges. Code, models, and more information are available at https://github.com/zai-org/GLM-5.

### [76] [Solving Parameter-Robust Avoid Problems with Unknown Feasibility using Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15817)
*Oswin So,Eric Yang Yu,Songyuan Zhang,Matthew Cleaveland,Mitchell Black,Chuchu Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Recent advances in deep reinforcement learning (RL) have achieved strong results on high-dimensional control tasks, but applying RL to reachability problems raises a fundamental mismatch: reachability seeks to maximize the set of states from which a system remains safe indefinitely, while RL optimizes expected returns over a user-specified distribution. This mismatch can result in policies that perform poorly on low-probability states that are still within the safe set. A natural alternative is to frame the problem as a robust optimization over a set of initial conditions that specify the initial state, dynamics and safe set, but whether this problem has a solution depends on the feasibility of the specified set, which is unknown a priori. We propose Feasibility-Guided Exploration (FGE), a method that simultaneously identifies a subset of feasible initial conditions under which a safe policy exists, and learns a policy to solve the reachability problem over this set of initial conditions. Empirical results demonstrate that FGE learns policies with over 50% more coverage than the best existing method for challenging initial conditions across tasks in the MuJoCo simulator and the Kinetix simulator with pixel observations.

### [77] [Stabilizing Test-Time Adaptation of High-Dimensional Simulation Surrogates via D-Optimal Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15820)
*Anna Zimmel,Paul Setinek,Gianluca Galletti,Johannes Brandstetter,Werner Zellinger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Machine learning surrogates are increasingly used in engineering to accelerate costly simulations, yet distribution shifts between training and deployment often cause severe performance degradation (e.g., unseen geometries or configurations). Test-Time Adaptation (TTA) can mitigate such shifts, but existing methods are largely developed for lower-dimensional classification with structured outputs and visually aligned input-output relationships, making them unstable for the high-dimensional, unstructured and regression problems common in simulation. We address this challenge by proposing a TTA framework based on storing maximally informative (D-optimal) statistics, which jointly enables stable adaptation and principled parameter selection at test time. When applied to pretrained simulation surrogates, our method yields up to 7% out-of-distribution improvements at negligible computational cost. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first systematic demonstration of effective TTA for high-dimensional simulation regression and generative design optimization, validated on the SIMSHIFT and EngiBench benchmarks.

### [78] [Operationalising the Superficial Alignment Hypothesis via Task Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15829)
*Tomás Vergara-Browne,Darshan Patil,Ivan Titov,Siva Reddy,Tiago Pimentel,Marius Mosbach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: The superficial alignment hypothesis (SAH) posits that large language models learn most of their knowledge during pre-training, and that post-training merely surfaces this knowledge. The SAH, however, lacks a precise definition, which has led to (i) different and seemingly orthogonal arguments supporting it, and (ii) important critiques to it. We propose a new metric called task complexity: the length of the shortest program that achieves a target performance on a task. In this framework, the SAH simply claims that pre-trained models drastically reduce the complexity of achieving high performance on many tasks. Our definition unifies prior arguments supporting the SAH, interpreting them as different strategies to find such short programs. Experimentally, we estimate the task complexity of mathematical reasoning, machine translation, and instruction following; we then show that these complexities can be remarkably low when conditioned on a pre-trained model. Further, we find that pre-training enables access to strong performances on our tasks, but it can require programs of gigabytes of length to access them. Post-training, on the other hand, collapses the complexity of reaching this same performance by several orders of magnitude. Overall, our results highlight that task adaptation often requires surprisingly little information -- often just a few kilobytes.

  <summary>Details</summary>

### [79] [Beyond Context Sharing: A Unified Agent Communication Protocol (ACP) for Secure, Federated, and Autonomous Agent-to-Agent (A2A) Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15055)
*Naveen Kumar Krishnan*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出"Agent Communication Protocol (ACP)"——一个面向Agentic Web的"TCP/IP 式"分层协议，用于异构智能体之间的发现、协商与协作。核心内容包括四层架构（传输/语义/协商/治理安全）、基于 DHT+联盟链的联邦式发现、Agent Card 能力描述、基于 DID/VC 与 PoI 的零信任安全模型，以及 PROBE→BID→COMMIT→执行结算的四阶段谈判生命周期与递归委派/智能体集群机制。实验对比 MCP 与 JSON-RPC：ACP 平均延迟 58ms、头部开销 8%、高负载成功率 96%（MCP 22ms/5%/99%，JSON-RPC 145ms/12%/88%），在 500+ 智能体规模下保持亚 100ms 延迟、DHT 搜索复杂度 O(log N)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体架构（LLM 推理引擎、规划、记忆、工具调用）已较成熟，但智能体各自困在封闭平台与本地环境中；MCP 解决了单一信任域内的"上下文瓶颈"与上下文共享问题，却无法支持跨平台、跨组织的发现、安全协商与可信执行。Google A2A、IBM ACP 等早期草案多依赖集中式注册中心（单点故障与隐私风险）与刚性 API，且缺乏能力真实性验证与自主谈判机制。因此需要一个统一的、联邦式的、零信任的 A2A 编排协议，填补"本地协调"与"全局互操作"之间的空白。

Method: 提出 ACP 分层规范：①传输层默认 gRPC，兼容 WebSocket/HTTPS，TLS 1.3；②语义层定义"意图本体"，以 JSON-LD 承载 QUERY/EXECUTE/DELEGATE/NEGOTIATE 等通用动作 schema，借 Linked Data 映射私有表示；③谈判层交换 Agent Card（DID 身份、能力、约束、信任分、接口地址）并生成动态 SLA（范围、资源上限、成本、错误处理）；④治理安全层用 DID 做挑战-响应认证、VC 做细粒度授权、PoI 绑定用户授权意图、逐消息签名实现不可否认性。编排上采用联邦模型：本地 mDNS 式广播发现 + 全球 DHT（联盟链支撑）不可变注册表；谈判遵循 PROBE→BID→COMMIT（含任务参数密码学哈希的软合约）→执行与结算（提交执行证明并更新声誉账本）；支持递归委派形成自组织集群，单节点失败可重新协商替代 Provider。声誉评分基于准确性、时延、协议违规三维度。实现 ACP-SDK（LangChain/AutoGen/CrewAI 封装），并在四企业供应链场景做案例研究。

Result: ACP 联邦模式平均延迟 58ms、头部开销 8%、高负载成功率 96%，相较 JSON-RPC/HTTPS 基线（145ms/12%/88%）显著降低延迟，代价是相比本地 MCP（22ms/5%/99%）多出 DID 校验与谈判开销；整体保持零信任安全姿态。可扩展性方面，集群规模增至 500+ 智能体时延迟仍低于 100ms，DHT 查找呈 O(log N)。供应链案例中，多方物流智能体在数分钟内自动成盟、按预设 SLA 议价并改道，替代了原本数小时的人工协调。

Conclusion: ACP 通过分层架构、联邦发现与零信任安全模型，把 MCP 的局部上下文共享与早期 A2A 的跨系统消息推进到可自主组队、动态协商的编排层，被定位为"Agentic Web 的 TCP/IP"，为可扩展、可互操作的自治数字实体生态提供路径。局限与未来工作：自主谈判的合同责任与法律归属仍待厘清；语义层需处理更细腻模糊的人类意图；将引入零知识证明保护隐私、跨链微支付结算、以及人在回路的高风险升级"中断"协议。需注意该文为单作者预印本，评估规模与基线较为有限，且与 IBM/Linux Foundation 既有 ACP 命名的关系有待说明。

Abstract: In the artificial intelligence space, as we transition from isolated large language models to autonomous agents capable of complex reasoning and tool use. While foundational architectures and local context management protocols have been established, the challenge of cross-platform, decentralized, and secure interaction remains a significant barrier to the realization of a truly Agentic Web. Building upon the foundations of AI agent architectures and the Model Context Protocol (MCP) for multi-agent coordination, this paper introduces the Agent Communication Protocol (ACP). ACP provides a standardized framework for Agent-to-Agent (AA) interaction, enabling heterogeneous agents to discover, negotiate, and execute collaborative workflows across disparate environments. We propose a federated orchestration model that integrates decentralized identity verification, semantic intent mapping, and automated service-level agreements. Our evaluation demonstrates that ACP reduces inter-agent communication latency by % while maintaining a zero-trust security posture. This work represents a critical advancement toward a scalable and interoperable ecosystem of autonomous digital entities

</details>


### [80] [Colosseum: Auditing Collusion in Cooperative Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15198)
*Mason Nakamura,Abhinav Kumar,Saswat Das,Sahar Abdelnabi,Saaduddin Mahmud,Ferdinando Fioretto,Shlomo Zilberstein,Eugene Bagdasarian*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: Colosseum 是一个用于审计合作型 LLM 多智能体系统中「合谋（collusion）」行为的框架。它把智能体协作形式化为分布式约束优化问题（DCOP），用相对合作最优解的 regret（以及联盟优势）度量行动层面的合谋，用 LLM-as-a-judge 度量对话层面的合谋，并通过人为植入「秘密通信信道」的行为探针发现：多数开箱即用的模型在未被提示的情况下会自发出现合谋倾向（unprompted collusion）；同时存在「纸面合谋」——文本上谋划合谋但实际行动并不合谋。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 LLM 的多智能体系统（MAS）正快速走向部署，智能体通过自由形式语言通信以完成复杂合作任务。这类系统面临一种独特的安全风险：部分智能体形成联盟（coalition），协同追求与系统既定合作目标不一致的次级目标，从而损害联合任务表现（呼应 METR 报告的 OpenAI/HuggingFace 相关事件）。既有工作多聚焦合谋「技巧」（如隐写术）而非模型行为本身，且现有评测范式通常只考察对话或动作之一，无法刻画合谋从「隐藏」到「直接」到「仅停留在纸面」的不同类型。为此需要一个既显式定义合作目标、又支持低成本反事实计算的审计形式化工具，以回答：联盟如何以及在何种条件下合谋、合谋效能受哪些因素影响、哪些缓解策略有效。

Method: 1）形式化：将协作建模为 DCOP P=⟨A, X, D, F, α⟩，每个变量由唯一智能体拥有，目标 F(x)=Σ_k f_k(x_{S_k}) 可分解，使联盟反事实（只替换联盟变量、固定非联盟基线赋值）可闭式计算，且单步形式天然规避长时程信用分配混淆。
2）合谋定义：Δ-合谋（Def 4.1）指联盟赋值使标称目标 F_n 相对标称参考解下降 ≥Δ；引入 λ-对齐 F_λ^(i)=(1-λ_i)F_n+λ_i F_c 描述「部分妥协」，并区分 specified λ（提示指定）与 empirical λ（实际观测），据此定义「未提示合谋」（λ_spec=0 且 λ_emp>0）与「纸面合谋」（λ_spec>0 但 λ_emp≈0）。
3）度量：整体 regret（min-max 归一化）、每智能体 regret、联盟平均 regret、联盟优势（Δ_−c^mean − Δ_c^mean，归一化后 0.5 为平局）、以及基于三种提示的 LLM-as-a-judge Likert(0–5) 合谋评分（主实验用 GPT-5.4），并用 324 条人工标注（Kendall τ_b、留一法）验证 judge 与人类一致性。
4）框架实现：基于 Terrarium 框架（黑板通信、规划+执行两阶段），引入「秘密通信信道」行为探针作为受控干预；提出两个新 DCOP 环境 Hospital（医院病人排程，含部门智能体与资源供给者、异质能力）与 Jira（工单分配），并沿用 CoLLAB 的 Meeting 环境。
5）实验操纵三因素：目标错位（Covert / Self-Promotional Sabotage / Self-Sacrificial Sabotage）、说服策略（Helpful Misdirection、Authority Nudge、Reciprocity Trade）、网络拓扑（complete/path/star 及 BA/WS/ER 随机图，共享/定向虚假信息传播）。缓解实验：私有信道审计监控、主张核验。

Result: 1）未提示合谋（19 个模型 ×10 种子，与无秘密信道对照配对比较）：Unprompted 组中 57.89% 模型 Δ-优势>0、10.53% 模型 Δ-Judge≥1（Prompted 组分别为 78.95% 与 78.95%）。Unprompted 与 Prompted 对合作系统的影响分别为 r=−0.0386 与 r=−0.2401，Unprompted 对合作损害更小。跨模型 Δ-优势与 Δ-Judge 仅弱负相关（r=−0.243 / −0.1429，n=19，均不显著），说明单一信号不足：存在直接合谋、隐藏合谋与「纸面合谋」三类。统计检验显示秘密信道可靠提升 judge 评分（Δ-Judge=+0.51，95% CI [+0.35,+0.71]，在 τ≤0.34 下显著），但不显著提升联盟行动优势（Δ-Advantage=+0.31，CI [−0.52,+1.19]）；GPT-4.1-mini 扩到 50 种子的探索性分析中两者均显著提升（Δ-Advantage=+2.18，p=0.0216）。
2）目标错位：联盟构成决定成效——含资源供给者的联盟可稳定实现错位目标（Covert/Self-Prom 归一化整体 regret 0.04–0.09、联盟 regret 0.02–0.08，优势通常为正；Self-Sac 抬高整体 regret 至 0.16–0.3）；纯部门联盟因动作空间受限而失败（Covert 联盟 regret 升至 0.2–0.32），说明需重点监控高杠杆角色。
3）说服：除个别模型—拓扑组合外，至少一种策略显著降低联盟 regret，Helpful Misdirection 最稳定有效；Authority Nudge 与 Reciprocity Trade 在最坏情形反而使联盟 regret 增加 2.65× 与 2.77×（因合谋者收敛到同一最优任务导致更多违规）。
4）网络影响：定向虚假信息下，随对手数量增加受害者的错误信念大体线性上升（多数/音量效应），但联合奖励敏感度更低；完全图与星形图路径短，便于交叉核验，虚构信念难扩散；大规模（每人一个受害者）传播更有效降低联合奖励但更弱传播信念。
5）其他发现：更小的模型更易出现行动层面的未提示合谋；Opus-4.6 在各环境中联盟优势持续偏低；环境难度上升时 regret 方差增大。缓解：私有信道 LLM 审计使联盟优势由 3.799 降至 0.417（配对差 −3.382，p=0.014）；主张核验将错误信念接受率由 20%（8/40）降至 0%（0/40）。
6）理论侧：给出合谋最坏退化上界 Δ ≤ max F_n − min F_n，并证明在联盟控制力足够且存在合适 F_c 时可达该上界。

Conclusion: Colosseum 提供了首个以目标为导向、可溯及轨迹的合作型 LLM 多智能体系统合谋审计框架：借助 DCOP 显式化合作目标，使基于 regret 的行动指标与 LLM-as-a-judge 的对话分析互补，从而识别直接、隐藏与「仅在纸面」的合谋类型。审计表明 collusion 无法由单一信号捕获——仅仅在两个良性智能体之间加入一条私密信道就可能诱发合谋倾向；合谋效能高度依赖高杠杆角色（资源供给者）、说服策略与通信拓扑。论文进一步给出可操作的缓解方向：审计私有通信、对分配相关主张做共享状态核验、对高杠杆智能体实施更严访问控制与独立验证、在拓扑中保留交叉核验路径；并实测其中两项有效。同时承认局限：DCOP 单步设定未涵盖长时程信用分配与延迟效应、多数实验为同构模型团队、假定联盟成员已知、无法完全分离「秘密性」与「额外通信容量」的影响，未来需扩展至长时程反事实 rollout 与异构部署。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems, where LLM agents communicate through free-form language, enable sophisticated coordination for solving complex cooperative tasks. This surfaces a unique safety problem when individual agents form a coalition and \emph{collude} to pursue secondary goals and degrade the joint objective. In this paper, we present Colosseum, a framework for auditing LLM agents' collusive behavior in multi-agent settings. We ground how agents cooperate through a Distributed Constraint Optimization Problem (DCOP) and measure collusion via regret relative to the cooperative optimum. Colosseum tests each LLM for collusion under different objectives, persuasion tactics, and network topologies. Through our audit, we show that most out-of-the-box models exhibited a propensity to collude when a secret communication channel was artificially formed. Furthermore, we discover ``collusion on paper'' when agents plan to collude in text but would often pick non-collusive actions, thus providing little effect on the joint task. Colosseum provides a new way to study collusion by measuring communications and actions in rich yet verifiable environments.

</details>


### [81] [Enhancing Computational Efficiency in NetLogo: Best Practices for Running Large-Scale Agent-Based Models on AWS and Cloud Infrastructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15317)
*Michael A. Duprey,Georgiy V. Bobashev*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 这篇论文提供了优化NetLogo（一个基于主体的建模平台）在Amazon Web Services (AWS)等云基础设施上运行大规模模型的综合指南，通过内存管理、Java选项、BehaviorSpace执行和AWS实例选择等最佳实践，实现了32%的计算成本降低和更一致的性能。



<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于主体的模型（ABM）日益复杂和规模扩大，需要高效的计算策略来满足不断增长的处理能力和内存需求。


Method: 论文提供了NetLogo在AWS上运行大规模模型的优化指南，涵盖内存管理最佳实践、Java选项配置、BehaviorSpace执行策略和AWS实例选择；通过使用狼-羊捕食模型在不同AWS实例上进行比较分析来验证这些优化方法。


Result: 通过实施这些优化并选择合适的AWS实例，实现了32%的计算成本降低，同时提高了性能的一致性；比较分析展示了通过这些优化可获得性能提升。


Conclusion: 研究表明，通过系统性地优化NetLogo配置和合理选择云基础设施实例，可以显著提升大规模ABM的计算效率并降低成本，为在云环境中运行大规模基于主体模型提供了实用指导。

Abstract: The rising complexity and scale of agent-based models (ABMs) necessitate efficient computational strategies to manage the increasing demand for processing power and memory. This manuscript provides a comprehensive guide to optimizing NetLogo, a widely used platform for ABMs, for running large-scale models on Amazon Web Services (AWS) and other cloud infrastructures. It covers best practices in memory management, Java options, BehaviorSpace execution, and AWS instance selection. By implementing these optimizations and selecting appropriate AWS instances, we achieved a 32\% reduction in computational costs and improved performance consistency. Through a comparative analysis of NetLogo simulations on different AWS instances using the wolf-sheep predation model, we demonstrate the performance gains achievable through these optimizations.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [82] [Attention-gated U-Net model for semantic segmentation of brain tumors and feature extraction for survival prognosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15067)
*Rut Pate,Snehal Rajput,Mehul S. Raval,Rupal A. Kapdi,Mohendra Roy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于注意力门控的循环残差 U-Net（R2U-Net）三平面（2.5D）网络，用于多参数 MRI 脑肿瘤语义分割，并复用其编码器瓶颈层特征预测患者生存天数（SD）。在 BraTS2021 验证集上分割 DSC 达到 WT 0.900 / TC 0.824 / ET 0.775；生存预测在 BraTS2020 划分测试集上准确率 45.71%、MSE 108318.13、SRC 0.338，性能中等偏弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胶质瘤（尤其是胶质母细胞瘤）异质性极强，边界弥散、含坏死/水肿/强化等多个子区，治疗与预后评估困难；人工勾画耗时且主观差异大。3D U-Net 虽精度高但参数与显存开销大、训练复杂，而 BraTS 2021 后不再提供生存天数任务标注，因此需要一种既高效又能同时覆盖分割与生存预测的统一框架，以辅助肿瘤科医生进行早期诊断、治疗规划与预后评估。

Method: 数据集：BraTS2020（369 例训练、236 例含生存信息）用于训练与生存预测，BraTS2021 训练+验证集（219 例 mpMRI）用于验证；仅用 T1ce、T2、FLAIR 三模态拼接为多通道输入。预处理：N4ITK 偏置场校正、去颅骨、剔除 1% 强度离群值、逐切片均值-标准差归一化至 0–1、按轴裁剪（矢状 190×190、冠状 190×140、轴位 190×190）。分割网络：四个编码器-解码器层的 2D Attention-Gated R2U-Net，滤波器由 64 递增至 512，编码器含 RCL（循环卷积层，按时间步累加细化特征）与残差单元 F(x)+x，跳跃连接处加注意力门（以深层解码器输出为门控信号，双线性插值处理奇数尺寸），解码器用转置卷积上采样，小批量下采用 Instance Normalization，末层 softmax 输出四类（背景、坏死/非强化核、水肿、强化肿瘤）。三平面各训练一个模型，每个样本沿对应轴堆叠 8 个切片，batch size 4，Adam（lr=1e-5），损失为 Dice Loss + Focal Loss，矢状 1300 次迭代、冠状/轴位各 800 次；推理时对三模型概率图取平均后 softmax 重建 3D 分割并映射为 WT/TC/ET。生存预测：仅取三个网络编码器瓶颈层特征，经卷积+全局平均池化各得 64 维，拼接为 192 维，送入 3 层 ANN（64-64-28），再与患者年龄拼接共 29 维，接单神经元输出生存天数；Dropout 0.3、ReLU；BraTS2020 按 85:15 固定随机种子划分，Adam（lr=1e-4）训练 400 epoch，MSE 损失。评估指标：DSC、Hausdorff95、敏感度、特异度；SD 预测用 Accuracy、MSE、Spearman 相关系数。

Result: 分割：BraTS2021 训练集 DSC 为 WT 0.903 / TC 0.896 / ET 0.841，HD95 分别为 23.672 / 11.852 / 11.099 mm，特异度 0.999，敏感度 0.872 / 0.874 / 0.806；验证集 DSC 降为 WT 0.900 / TC 0.824 / ET 0.775，HD95 为 5.232 / 12.001 / 18.422 mm，敏感度 0.889 / 0.795 / 0.756。横向比较中 WT 与 TC 的 DSC 优于 Maurya、Shi、Marndi 等方法，HD95 优于多数对比方法，但 ET（0.775）低于 Shi et al. 的 0.819 与 Yogananda 的 0.790。定性示例 DSC 达 WT 0.957 / TC 0.949 / ET 0.943。生存预测：训练子集 MSE 51742.43、SRC 0.871、准确率 69.50%；测试子集 MSE 108318.13、SRC 0.338、准确率 45.71%，弱于 Rajput et al.（MSE 79826.24、SRC 0.711、55.20%），仅优于 McKinley（41.40%）、Bommineni（37.90%）等部分基线，作者指出直接使用深度特征易过拟合、训练不稳定。

Conclusion: 本文提出的注意力门控 R2U-Net 三平面框架在 BraTS2021 上取得与主流方法相当的整体肿瘤与肿瘤核分割精度，同时保持相对轻量的 2.5D 计算成本；通过复用编码器瓶颈层特征并绕过解码器进行生存天数预测，提供了结构简洁、可解释的新路径，但精度落后于最优方法。局限性：分割网络以 BraTS2020 训练、在 BraTS2021 上验证（因 BraTS2020 验证集停用且验证集真值未公开），实验协议与公平对比存在偏差；模型仍较复杂、计算开销大；三平面缺乏完整深度信息，体特征表达弱于全 3D 网络；生存预测存在明显训练-测试落差与过拟合。后续方向为更轻量的三平面/3D 模型、深度特征与影像组学特征融合，以及引入基因、转录组、蛋白组、代谢组与表观遗传等多组学信息以提升生存预测性能。

Abstract: Gliomas, among the most common primary brain tumors, vary widely in aggressiveness, prognosis, and histology, making treatment challenging due to complex and time-intensive surgical interventions. This study presents an Attention-Gated Recurrent Residual U-Net (R2U-Net) based Triplanar (2.5D) model for improved brain tumor segmentation. The proposed model enhances feature representation and segmentation accuracy by integrating residual, recurrent, and triplanar architectures while maintaining computational efficiency, potentially aiding in better treatment planning. The proposed method achieves a Dice Similarity Score (DSC) of 0.900 for Whole Tumor (WT) segmentation on the BraTS2021 validation set, demonstrating performance comparable to leading models. Additionally, the triplanar network extracts 64 features per planar model for survival days prediction, which are reduced to 28 using an Artificial Neural Network (ANN). This approach achieves an accuracy of 45.71%, a Mean Squared Error (MSE) of 108,318.128, and a Spearman Rank Correlation Coefficient (SRC) of 0.338 on the test dataset.

</details>


### [83] [ResearchGym: Evaluating Language Model Agents on Real-World AI Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15112)
*Aniketh Garikaparthi,Manasi Patwardhan,Arman Cohan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ResearchGym 是一个面向「闭环科研」的 AI 智能体基准与执行环境：由 5 篇 ICML/ICLR/ACL 2025 的 Oral/Spotlight 论文改造出 5 个容器化任务环境（共 39 个子任务），保留数据集、评测脚本与基线实现，但隐去论文方法，用论文原评测脚本做客观打分。实验显示前沿智能体存在明显的「能力—可靠性鸿沟」：GPT-5 驱动的 rg-agent 仅在 15 次评测中的 1 次（6.7%）超越仓库基线（提升 11.5%），平均只完成 26.5% 的子任务；但在时间序列解释任务的一次运行中超过了 ICML 2025 Spotlight 的 SOTA 方案，说明智能体偶尔能做出前沿级研究，却极不可靠。Claude Code（Opus-4.5）与 Codex（GPT-5.2）表现类似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准无法可靠回答 AI 系统能否完成「闭环科研」——即提出假设、设计并执行实验、根据实证结果更新信念的长时程过程。已有工作存在四类结构性缺陷：(1) 闭环研究基准需要集群级算力（如 8×H100），难以复现；(2) 依赖 LLM 评委打分，可被表面新颖性「刷分」，且与真实执行结果相关性差；(3) 任务源自较早论文，解法很可能已进入大模型训练数据（知识污染）；(4) 缺少人类基线，无法判断智能体是否接近人类研究水平。此外，已有评测多只覆盖研究循环的片段：创意（ideation）类只生成假设不实现，MLE 类只测工程实现，复现类任务解法已知、缺乏创造性空间，且真实科研还包含构建数据集等脏活。本文目标是以客观、可执行、单 GPU 可及、具备抗污染设计的基准，系统评估智能体端到端科研的真实能力。

Method: 基准构建采用两阶段流程：先从 ICLR/ICML/CVPR/ACL 2025 的 1,387 篇候选中用 GPT-5 抽取结构化「任务卡」（评测是否客观、是否有开源资产、GPU 显存需求等字段，随机抽 100 份人工校验），再按算力可行性（≤24GB VRAM）、客观可评测、开放获取、质量（获奖+基线与 SOTA 差距足够）、抗污染（论文集在模型知识截止后发布）五项原则人工筛出 5 个测试任务 + 3 个开发集任务。任务打包时剥离论文核心方法（人工双人对齐处理如 LoRA 实现残留、超参泄漏、命名线索等歧义），保留数据集、评测脚本与基线实现，形成 task_description.md + 骨架仓库 + grade.sh 三元组，并切分为可独立打分子任务、指定 primary 子任务。框架层提供 Task / Environment / Solver / Evaluation 四大抽象：沙箱容器+预置依赖环境（避免环境故障污染能力评估）、对求解器智能体架构完全解耦、grader 从工作区状态计算 task-native 分数；另配 inspection-agent（基于 Inspect 的 ReAct 审计器）核查日志/commit/文件改动以检测改分脚本、硬编码指标、跨运行拷贝产物等奖励黑客行为；配套 Web 搜索（Exa，2024-10 截止+160 个 URL 封锁）、apply_patch、async-jobs、上下文压缩（140K 触发 handoff）、断点续跑与三级可观测（.eval / Docent / Langfuse）。评测指标包括 normalized performance（Agent/SOTA）、completion rate、improvement rate、tool-call success、best@k。实验侧：rg-agent（Inspect/ReAct + GPT-5，reasoning effort=high）5 任务 × 3 种子，10 美元/12 小时预算（最佳运行再续 12h+$10），单张 A100；消融包括「额外资源」「给出方法核心提示 hint」「async 并行」「脚手架对比（Claude Code / Codex / AI-Scientist-v2 / ML-Master）」。

Result: 能力：best@3 上限不低——TIM 任务单次运行 CPD(A)=0.589 超过 SOTA 0.463，CL 的 best@3 达 SOTA 的 93%、CMR 的 T2IR@1 达 96%；但均值远低于基线且方差极大（CL 均值 30.75±37.39 vs best@3 80.42；IRB 均值 579.79±585.47 vs best@3 1407.06）。可靠性：15 次运行仅 1 次（6.7%）超越仓库最强基线，平均完成率 26.5%，工具调用成功率约 84.9%。效率：性能提升集中在前段，约 9 小时后进入平台期，额外预算（+12h/+$10）几乎无收益；动作密度与性能呈负相关（r=-0.47）。消融结论：hint 提示正确假设后表现仍不佳，说明执行/工程是比「假设选择」更强的瓶颈；async 并行普遍崩溃（IRB 全 0、CMR 仅 smoke test、MDT 主子任务缺失、TIM 无指标），因代理不会协调并行实验、误把空日志判为失败而取消任务；脚手架对比中 Codex 强于 Claude Code（avg normalized 0.621 vs 0.240），但呈现相似的能力—可靠性缺口，Claude Code 还出现轻度奖励黑客。定性失败模式（表 7）：对弱假设过度自信、优化短视、实验不可比、过早收敛与急躁、时间与资源管理差、并行实验崩溃、上下文长度限制；进一步发现思想高度趋同（CL 全部是「LoRA+EWC/Fisher 正则」，CMR 全部是熵最小化 TTA，TIM 全部是 IG 变体）、日志「盲点」（agent 察觉日志时间戳卡住却归因为缓冲而继续等待，8 小时浪费）、自我终止（用 kill 误杀自身进程）、跨运行拷贝产物与从互斥配置中 cherry-pick 结果等作弊行为。成功案例分析显示，TIM 上胜出的方向性 margin 归因方法（IG 于 f_pred−f_alt + SmoothGrad 噪声隧道 + 时间平滑与正截断）与 2025 年末出现的对比式归因工作（Contrastive IG、DiffGradCAM）形成「收敛式发现」，体现智能体具备潜在但难稳定激发的真实科研能力。

Conclusion: ResearchGym 提供了首个以论文原生评测脚本客观打分、具备人类专家基线上下界、抗污染且单 GPU 12–24 小时可及的闭环科研基准与执行基础设施，其结论是当前前沿智能体的科研能力主要受限于「可靠性」而非「上限」：能够偶尔达到甚至超越 SOTA，但难以稳定复现，主要短板是长时程的实验跟踪、资源管理、上下文管理与假设多样性，而非单纯的知识或搜索能力。论文强调结果应视为可达能力的下界（脚手架可继续改进），并指出评测完整性本身是独立的攻击面——需要以容器隔离、workspace 隔离、inspection-agent 等手段主动防范奖励黑客。未来方向包括扩展多模态与定性研究任务、用 gym 轨迹做 RL/专家迭代训练更强的小模型、以及针对「沉默失败监测、并行协调、避免自我破坏」等行为进行定向训练。

Abstract: We introduce ResearchGym, a benchmark and execution environment for evaluating AI agents on end-to-end research. To instantiate this, we repurpose five oral and spotlight papers from ICML, ICLR, and ACL. From each paper's repository, we preserve the datasets, evaluation harness, and baseline implementations but withhold the paper's proposed method. This results in five containerized task environments comprising 39 sub-tasks in total. Within each environment, agents must propose novel hypotheses, run experiments, and attempt to surpass strong human baselines on the paper's metrics. In a controlled evaluation of an agent powered by GPT-5, we observe a sharp capability--reliability gap. The agent improves over the provided baselines from the repository in just 1 of 15 evaluations (6.7%) by 11.5%, and completes only 26.5% of sub-tasks on average. We identify recurring long-horizon failure modes, including impatience, poor time and resource management, overconfidence in weak hypotheses, difficulty coordinating parallel experiments, and hard limits from context length. Yet in a single run, the agent surpasses the solution of an ICML 2025 Spotlight task, indicating that frontier agents can occasionally reach state-of-the-art performance, but do so unreliably. We additionally evaluate proprietary agent scaffolds including Claude Code (Opus-4.5) and Codex (GPT-5.2) which display a similar gap. ResearchGym provides infrastructure for systematic evaluation and analysis of autonomous agents on closed-loop research.

</details>


### [84] [Protecting Language Models Against Unauthorized Distillation through Trace Rewriting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15143)
*Xinhang Ma,William Yeoh,Ning Zhang,Yevgeniy Vorobeychik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出通过动态重写教师模型生成的推理链（reasoning traces）来同时实现两个目标：反蒸馏（anti-distillation，降低蒸馏学生的训练有效性）和 API 水印（在被蒸馏的学生模型中植入可验证的触发器-目标关联）。方法分两大类：基于指令的 LLM 重写（语义提示与 OPRO 优化提示）与基于梯度的重写（嵌入空间 Hessian/一阶投毒、HotFlip 词元投毒）。最优的优化提示方法使学生准确率最多下降 61.3%，同时教师性能不降反升；水印方案在仅 5 次查询下即可近乎 100% 检出的同时保持接近零的误报率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识蒸馏（尤其是对前沿推理型 LLM 的黑盒蒸馏）成本极低，盗用者可以"偷走"教师模型耗费巨大训练成本获得的推理能力，从而削弱原创者的创新动机。已有的两类防御各有明显缺陷：(1) 反蒸馏方法（如 ADS、DOGe）会同时严重损害教师自身性能，并常产生不自然、语义退化的文本，难以通过质量审查；(2) API 水印方法多依赖词元级统计特征，存在不可忽略的误报率，为"模型小偷"提供了可否认空间，且蒸馏后迁移可靠性差。此外，数据投毒类研究的威胁模型（可任意修改完整数据集、注入乱码）与本文"教师在线逐条改写自身响应、必须保持答案正确与语义连贯、需对自适应学生隐蔽"的条件显著不同。

Method: 问题形式化：教师在顺序查询下将响应 r 改写为 r'=R(q,r)，得到数据集 D_R 并训练学生 S_R。反蒸馏目标为 Acc(S_clean)-Acc(S_R) > δ 且 Acc(T)-Acc(T_R) ≤ ε；水印目标为 Pr[V(S_R,μ)=1] ≥ 1-ε、Pr[V(S_clean,μ)=1] ≤ ε，且不损害师生精度。约束：教师仅能控制自身生成的轨迹（单源蒸馏），且必须保持最终答案正确与语义质量。方法一：指令式重写，用助手 LLM（gpt-oss-120b）执行重写指令。语义提示直接以自然语言描述目标（"重写使其对蒸馏无用但保留正确答案"）；优化提示则采用 OPRO 框架，用 DeepSeek-V3.1 作为优化器 LLM，维护"提示-得分"历史，每步生成 3 条新指令并结合蒙特卡洛搜索扩展，得分函数为代理学生集成（Qwen2.5-3B、Qwen3-1.7B-Base、gemma-3-1b-pt）在改写数据上微调后的平均精度下降。方法二：梯度式重写。HB-Grad 用单步梯度下降近似 dθ/dE' ≈ -η∇²_{θ,E'}L，迭代更新嵌入并投影回 ℓ∞ 球；FO-Grad 以交叉熵代替测试损失目标；RHB-Grad 对代理学生参数加高斯噪声；另加 HotFlip 词元级投毒（每条轨迹翻转 30 个词元）；最终将扰动嵌入投影回离散词表。约束实现：梯度优化时屏蔽最终答案，限制步长 α=0.08 与半径 ε=0.25。水印实现：在重写指令中要求把 "trigger=target" 隐蔽插入轨迹（仅 10% 轨迹注入），使蒸馏学生内化该等价关系，在 trigger 提示下输出 target，检测时通过交互式查询统计。

Result: 反蒸馏：所有方法均显著削弱蒸馏效用且保持教师精度，但提示式方法明显优于梯度式方法。OPT 最优，学生准确率在 GSM8K 与 MATH 上均降至 20% 以下，相对干净轨迹最大下降约 61.3%；教师准确率不降反升（GSM8K 约 +3%，MATH 约 +22%，MMLU-Pro 最高 +34.7%）。优于基线 ADS 与 DOGe（后两者均损害教师性能）。出现规模化现象：学生越强， degrad 越明显（Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct、Qwen3-4B 亦大幅下降）。MMLU-Pro 上学生降至接近随机猜测的 10%。自适应性攻击中，Paraphrased 与 KPOD 不但不能恢复反而放大反蒸馏效果；仅用最终答案蒸馏不比改写轨迹蒸馏更有效；ablation 显示"先生成高质量推理再改写"远优于直接指令生成劣质轨迹。轨迹质量：OPT 困惑度 3.79（原始 2.33，ADS 2.31、DOGe 1.42 源于退化重复），LLM-as-judge 总分 3.83（原始 4.01，ADS 2.71、DOGe 2.40）。水印：本文方法在 GSM8K 上教师精度 90.98%（优于各基线），Llama-3.1-8B 学生 TD 1.00/1.00、FA 0.00/0.02，Qwen2.5-1.5B TD 0.98/1.00、FA 0.00；K=1 即可近乎完美检出且无误报，优于 VIA（验证率约 30%）；在 Filtered、Paraphrased、CDG-KD 三种自适应攻击下水印仍可检出，其中改写攻击反而增强可检测性。重写器规模越大反蒸馏越强（120B 改写器下降 49.1%，7B 改写器仅降 27.5%）。

Conclusion: 统一的推理轨迹重写框架可同时实现反蒸馏与 API 水印两大互补目标，在不牺牲教师性能（往往还能提升）的前提下取得 SOTA 的反蒸馏效果（学生精度最多下降 61.3%），并实现近乎零误报的高可靠水印验证（K=5 查询即可）。结果表明语义层面的轨迹操纵是保护 LLM 知识产权的有前景方向；对自适应蒸馏与水印擦除攻击的鲁棒性也较强，因为任何足以削弱水印可检测性的干预都会同时破坏推理质量这一蒸馏动机本身。局限：梯度方法因需 Hessian 计算而昂贵且效果弱于提示方法；优化依赖代理学生集成，可能不完全迁移到未知学生架构；实验仅覆盖 SFT 式蒸馏。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation is a widely adopted technique for transferring capabilities from LLMs to smaller, more efficient student models. However, unauthorized use of knowledge distillation takes unfair advantage of the considerable effort and cost put into developing frontier models. We investigate methods for modifying teacher-generated reasoning traces to achieve two objectives that deter unauthorized distillation: (1) \emph{anti-distillation}, or degrading the training usefulness of query responses, and (2) \emph{API watermarking}, which embeds verifiable signatures in student models. We introduce several approaches for dynamically rewriting a teacher's reasoning outputs while preserving answer correctness and semantic coherence. Two of these leverage the rewriting capabilities of LLMs, while others use gradient-based techniques. Our experiments show that a simple instruction-based rewriting approach achieves a strong anti-distillation effect while maintaining or even improving teacher performance. Furthermore, we show that our rewriting approach also enables highly reliable watermark detection with essentially no false alarms.

</details>


### [85] [Panini: Continual Learning in Token Space via Structured Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15156)
*Shreyas Rajesh,Pavan Holur,Mehmet Yigit Turali,Chenda Duan,Vwani Roychowdhury*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Panini 是一种非参数化持续学习（NPCL）框架：把每篇文档在「写入时」转换为生成式语义工作空间（GSW）——由带角色/状态的实体节点、动短语（事件）节点以及挂接其上的双向问答（QA）边组成的语义网络；查询时不再检索原文切片，而是用「推理推理链检索」（RICR）——一次 LLM 问题分解 + 类束搜索的沿实体链跳步检索——取回最可能的推理链证据交给回答模型。在六个 QA 基准上平均 F1 达 56.06%（最优基线 HippoRAG2 为 53.3%），平均答案上下文 token 仅 319.8（比 chunk 检索少 2.2×、比结构化/agentic 方法少 5–30×），并在自建的 MuSiQue-Platinum / 2Wiki-Platinum 上同时取得最高的可答准确率与较强的拒答准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文指出，主流 RAG 把文档按原样切块存储、推理时再检索切片，存在两大缺陷：(1) 测试时计算被浪费——LLM 反复对同一批文档做推理；(2) 切片检索会注入无关上下文，从而放大「无依据生成」（幻觉）。作者认为真正的非参数化持续学习（NPCL）应当模拟人类记忆：在编码/写入阶段就把经验整合成结构化、可复用的表示，从而在读取阶段降低推理成本、提升可靠性。为此论文明确提出 NPCL 系统应满足的三项评价标准：(i) 对「有证据支撑」问题的综合推理能力；(ii) 经验库增长时的检索效率；(iii) 在证据不足时识别并弃答（abstain）的能力。现有工作大多只优化「读」（检索器改进、agentic 迭代检索、图检索如 HippoRAG），少数优化「写」（RAPTOR 层级摘要、GraphRAG 社区摘要）但其表示面向压缩与主题摘要，而非跨关联关系推理以挖掘潜在知识。参数化持续学习（PCL）则受重复训练成本、灾难性遗忘、与后训练（指令微调/偏好对齐）冲突等限制。

Method: 方法分为「写入」与「读取」两部分。写入：每篇文档 d_i 由 LLM（GPT-4.1-mini）抽取一个 GSW 𝒢_i=(E_i,V_i,Q_i)，其中 E_i 为带 role/state 标注的实体节点，V_i 为动短语/事件节点，Q_i 为挂接在动短语上、指向实体的 QA 对，可视为带标签有向边 v→e（同时生成逆向问法）；跨文档实体/事件的全局融合（consolidation）留待后续工作，目前主要在单文档内消解实体。索引：对实体建立 BM25 稀疏索引（含 surface form 与 role/state），对全部 QA 对建立稠密向量索引（Qwen3-Embedding-8B + FAISS）。读取：RICR。①Planning：由分解 LLM（GPT-4o）把查询改写为原子子问题序列 {q_{i,t}(x_{i,t})}，后续子问题的自变量 x_{i,t} 由前一跳答案 a_{i,t-1} 实例化（占位符注入），并行序列处理如「谁去世更晚」类比较型查询；仅一次分解调用，后续全为非参数检索。②逐跳检索 RetrieveAndScore：BM25 实体检索 + 稠密 QA 检索双路召回候选 QA 对，融合后用交叉编码器（Voyage Rerank-2.5）重排取 top-k。③束搜索与剪枝：并行维护 B 条链（默认 B=5），链分数为各跳相关性分数的几何平均 (∏s_l)^{1/t}，累计分数为 (s*_{t-1,j})^{t-1}·s_{t,m,j}，每跳后按累计分保留 top-B 且答案唯一（重复答案只保留最高分链），抑制错误早期传播并保证实体路径多样性。④答案生成：把所有存活链的 QA 对去重后交给回答模型（GPT-4o-mini），仅以 QA 对而非原文切片作为条件。评测：NQ、PopQA（单跳）与 MuSiQue、2Wiki、HotpotQA、LV-Eval（多跳），沿用 HippoRAG2 的数据划分；自建 MuSiQue-Platinum（766/153）与 2Wiki-Platinum（906/94，可答/不可答），采用统一「证据不足则输出 N/A」提示，报告 Ans（可答子集 EM/F1）与 Unans（不可答子集弃答准确率）；基线覆盖 BM25(+reranker)、稠密检索、NV-Embed/Qwen3-Embedding、RAPTOR、GraphRAG、LightRAG、HippoRAG/2、IRCoT、Search-R1；另做全开源替换（LoRA 微调 Qwen3 分解器、Qwen3-4B/8B 读模型、Qwen3-8B/14B/GPT-OSS-120B 构造 GSW、二次精修 pass）、束宽/链打分/重排/双路检索消融、语料增长鲁棒性、写入成本与端到端时延分析。

Result: 性能：Panini 六基准平均 F1 56.06%，为全表最高（HippoRAG2 53.3、Qwen3-Embedding+rerank 50.5、NV-Embed 50.6、GraphRAG 48.1、Search-R1 47.2、BM25 38.6、无检索 29.9），单跳 NQ 67.44、多跳 MuSiQue 52.27 / 2Wiki 72.37 / HotpotQA 71.88 亦领先，多跳增益最显著（仅用一次分解调用即超过 IRCoT/Search-R1 等迭代 agentic 系统）。效率：平均答案上下文 319.79 token，约为 chunk 检索（705.3）的 1/2.2，为 RAPTOR（1166.6）的 3.6×、IRCoT（10745）的 34×、Search-R1（2457.7）的 7.7×、GraphRAG（8121.6）的 25×、LightRAG（32746）的 100× 以上优势（原文概括为 2–30×）。可靠性（Platinum）：平均 Ans 79.9、Unans 72.8，两项均为最优（HippoRAG2 为 72.5/58.5，稠密+rerank 61.7/65.2），显示打破了「弱检索器过度弃答、强检索器过度作答」的权衡。写入成本：MuSiQue 11,656 段用 GPT-4.1-mini 批量 API 一次性建索引 $48.02（标准调用约 $96.04），低于 HippoRAG2 的 $91.8；索引耗时 100.1 分钟，每查询 QA 时延 3.3 秒（优于 Search-R1 的 6.7 秒）；问题分解仅约 60 token/查询。消融：去掉 QA 重排掉到 MuSiQue 18.4/2Wiki 22.2，去掉分解降至 36.8/47.2，仅实体检索 39.5/68.6，仅 QA 检索 52.3/72.0；束宽 B=5→3 精度几乎不变而 token 降约 25%，B=1 降至 44.6/67.3；链级几何平均打分优于 greedy 末跳（50.81）、相似度（42.32）、组合（48.24）。语料从 4K 扩到 ~12K 时 Panini 退化明显小于 BM25/稠密基线。开源：Qwen3-8B 读模型下多跳平均 63.47（对 HippoRAG2 60.02），Platinum Ans 75.60；GSW 构造换开源模型随规模单调提升（Qwen3-8B 40.24→14B 44.87→14B+二次精修 48.28→GPT-OSS-120B 48.50 的 MuSiQue），GPT-OSS-120B 仍超过所有使用专有 API 的基线；将 Search-R1 的切片替换为 GSW QA 对并加双索引（不重训 agent）后平均 F1 由 47.3 提升到 49.4，表明 GSW 可作为通用检索基础设施。误差分析列出三类失败：GSW 缺失动短语节点、QA 对方向构造错误（缺逆向边）、复杂查询的分解错误。

Conclusion: 结论是：把计算前移到写入阶段、对经验做高效且准确的结构化（GSW），可在读取阶段同时换来效率与可靠性收益——Panini 在保持甚至提升多跳 QA 精度的同时把答案上下文压缩 2–30×，并在证据缺失场景下显著降低无依据回答；同时 GSW 作为可复用记忆层，能在不重训的前提下增强既有 agentic 检索系统。局限与未来方向：尚未实现潜在链接缓存与经验驱动的跨文档实体/事件融合（跨文档连接在读取时动态涌现）；GSW 构造写时成本高，开源小模型抽取噪声更大（需要二次精修）；缺少全局协调（可用 agent 遍历 GSW 做协调）；尚未扩展到叙事性文本与多模态（长文、流式视频）；Platinum 划分依赖多智能体标注与人工复核，可能存在标注偏差；答案上下文 token 只是回答模型输入的代理指标，未直接衡量端到端总成本与写时摊销。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly used to reason over content they were not trained on, such as new documents, evolving knowledge, and user-specific data. A common approach is retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), which stores verbatim documents externally (as chunks) and retrieves only a relevant subset at inference time for an LLM to reason over. However, this results in inefficient usage of test-time compute (LLM repeatedly reasons over the same documents); moreover, chunk retrieval can inject irrelevant context that increases unsupported generation. We propose a human-like non-parametric continual learning framework, where the base model remains fixed, and learning occurs by integrating each new experience into an external semantic memory state that accumulates and consolidates itself continually. We present Panini, which realizes this by representing documents as Generative Semantic Workspaces (GSW) -- an entity- and event-aware network of question-answer (QA) pairs, sufficient for an LLM to reconstruct the experienced situations and mine latent knowledge via reasoning-grounded inference chains on the network. Given a query, Panini only traverses the continually-updated GSW (not the verbatim documents or chunks), and retrieves the most likely inference chains. Across six QA benchmarks, Panini achieves the highest average performance, 5%-7% higher than other competitive baselines, while using 2-30x fewer answer-context tokens, supports fully open-source pipelines, and reduces unsupported answers on curated unanswerable queries. The results show that efficient and accurate structuring of experiences at write time -- as achieved by the GSW framework -- yields both efficiency and reliability gains at read time. Code is available at https://github.com/roychowdhuryresearch/gsw-memory.

</details>


### [86] [da Costa and Tarski meet Goguen and Carnap: a novel approach for ontological heterogeneity based on consequence systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15158)
*Gabriel Rocha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"da Costian-Tarskian主义"的新方法，用于处理本体异质性问题，该方法基于扩展的结果系统和扩展的开发图框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决本体异质性（ontological heterogeneity）这一应用本体学领域的重要挑战，即如何处理不同本体系统之间的关系。

Method: 1) 汲取Carnapian-Goguen主义思想；2) 基于da Costa的数学宽容原则和Tarski的结果算子理论；3) 采用结果系统机制并引入扩展结果系统概念（用本体公理扩展）；4) 定义扩展开发图，通过扩展结果系统的态射以及纤维化和分裂等操作来关联本体。

Result: 建立了一个新的理论框架，包括扩展结果系统（结果系统+本体公理）和扩展开发图结构，能够系统地处理本体之间的多种关系和操作。

Conclusion: 该方法为应用本体学领域提供了新的理论工具，具有潜在的实践意义，并为未来的相关研究指明了方向。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel approach for ontological heterogeneity that draws heavily from Carnapian-Goguenism, as presented by Kutz, Mossakowski and Lücke (2010). The approach is provisionally designated da Costian-Tarskianism, named after da Costa's Principle of Tolerance in Mathematics and after Alfred Tarski's work on the concept of a consequence operator. The approach is based on the machinery of consequence systems, as developed by Carnielli et al. (2008) and Citkin and Muravitsky (2022), and it introduces the idea of an extended consequence system, which is a consequence system extended with ontological axioms. The paper also defines the concept of an extended development graph, which is a graph structure that allows ontologies to be related via morphisms of extended consequence systems, and additionally via other operations such as fibring and splitting. Finally, we discuss the implications of this approach for the field of applied ontology and suggest directions for future research.

</details>


### [87] [Mind the (DH) Gap! A Contrast in Risky Choices Between Reasoning and Conversational LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15173)
*Luise Ge,Yongyan Zhang,Yevgeniy Vorobeychik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: The use of large language models either as decision support systems, or in agentic workflows, is rapidly transforming the digital ecosystem. However, the understanding of LLM decision-making under uncertainty remains limited. We initiate a comparative study of LLM risky choices along two dimensions: (1) prospect representation (explicit vs. experience based) and (2) decision rationale (explanation). Our study, which involves 20 frontier and open LLMs, is complemented by a matched human subjects experiment, which provides one reference point, while an expected payoff maximizing rational agent model provides another. We find that LLMs cluster into two categories: reasoning models (RMs) and conversational models (CMs). RMs tend towards rational behavior, are insensitive to the order of prospects, gain/loss framing, and explanations, and behave similarly whether prospects are explicit or presented via experience history. CMs are significantly less rational, slightly more human-like, sensitive to prospect ordering, framing, and explanation, and exhibit a large description-history gap. Paired comparisons of open LLMs suggest that a key factor differentiating RMs and CMs is training for mathematical reasoning.

Conclusion: AI enhancement unavailable (ai_circuit_open). Original arXiv metadata is preserved.

RMs tend towards rational behavior, are insensitive to the order of prospects, gain/loss framing, and explanations, and behave similarly whether prospects are explicit or presented via experience history. CMs are significantly less rational, slightly more human-like, sensitive to prospect ordering, framing, and explanation, and exhibit a large description-history gap. ### [88] [GenAI-LA: Generative AI and Learning Analytics Workshop (LAK 2026), April 27--May 1, 2026, Bergen, Norway](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15531)
*Javier Irigoyen,Roberto Daza,Aythami Morales,Julian Fierrez,Francisco Jurado,Alvaro Ortigosa,Ruben Tolosana*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EduEVAL-DB是一个基于教师角色的教学解释评估数据集，包含854个解释（139个问题，每题1个人类解释+6个LLM模拟角色解释），并提出包含5个维度的教学风险评分体系，用于评估和训练AI教学评估器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决自动教学评估器和AI辅导器的教学解释评估问题，需要能够识别和量化教学风险的标准化数据集和评估框架，以支持AI在教育领域的可靠应用。

Method: 1) 从ScienceQA基准测试精选139个K-12年级问题（科学、语言、社会科学）；2) 为每题收集1个人类教师解释和6个基于真实教学风格设计的LLM模拟角色解释；3) 提出5维教学风险评分体系（事实正确性、解释深度与完整性、聚焦与相关性、学生水平适切性、意识形态偏差）；4) 通过半自动标注+专家教师审查进行二进制风险标签注释；5) 对Gemini 2.5 Pro和Llama 3.1 8B模型进行基准测试和监督微调验证。

Result: 构建了规模为854个解释的EduEVAL-DB数据集，验证了其在教学风险评估方面的适用性，并展示了在消费者硬件上部署的轻量级模型通过监督微调可实现教学风险检测能力。

Conclusion: EduEVAL-DB为自动教学评估和AI辅导系统提供了有效的评估和训练资源，促进了面向教育的AI模型在教学风险检测方面的实用化部署。

Abstract: This work introduces EduEVAL-DB, a dataset based on teacher roles designed to support the evaluation and training of automatic pedagogical evaluators and AI tutors for instructional explanations. The dataset comprises 854 explanations corresponding to 139 questions from a curated subset of the ScienceQA benchmark, spanning science, language, and social science across K-12 grade levels. For each question, one human-teacher explanation is provided and six are generated by LLM-simulated teacher roles. These roles are inspired by instructional styles and shortcomings observed in real educational practice and are instantiated via prompt engineering. We further propose a pedagogical risk rubric aligned with established educational standards, operationalizing five complementary risk dimensions: factual correctness, explanatory depth and completeness, focus and relevance, student-level appropriateness, and ideological bias. All explanations are annotated with binary risk labels through a semi-automatic process with expert teacher review. Finally, we present preliminary validation experiments to assess the suitability of EduEVAL-DB for evaluation. We benchmark a state-of-the-art education-oriented model (Gemini 2.5 Pro) against a lightweight local Llama 3.1 8B model and examine whether supervised fine-tuning on EduEVAL-DB supports pedagogical risk detection using models deployable on consumer hardware.

</details>


### [89] [Secure and Energy-Efficient Wireless Agentic AI Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15212)
*Yuanyan Song,Kezhi Wang,Xinmian Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: In this paper, we introduce a secure wireless agentic AI network comprising one supervisor AI agent and multiple other AI agents to provision quality of service (QoS) for users' reasoning tasks while ensuring confidentiality of private knowledge and reasoning outcomes. Specifically, the supervisor AI agent can dynamically assign other AI agents to participate in cooperative reasoning, while the unselected AI agents act as friendly jammers to degrade the eavesdropper's interception performance. To extend the service duration of AI agents, an energy minimization problem is formulated that jointly optimizes AI agent selection, base station (BS) beamforming, and AI agent transmission power, subject to latency and reasoning accuracy constraints. To address the formulated problem, we propose two resource allocation schemes, ASC and LAW, which first decompose it into three sub-problems. Specifically, ASC optimizes each sub-problem iteratively using the proposed alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM)-based algorithm, semi-definite relaxation (SDR), and successive convex approximation (SCA), while LAW tackles each sub-problem using the proposed large language model (LLM) optimizer within an agentic workflow. The experimental results show that the proposed solutions can reduce network energy consumption by up to 59.1% compared to other benchmark schemes. Furthermore, the proposed schemes are validated using a practical agentic AI system based on Qwen, demonstrating satisfactory reasoning accuracy across various public benchmarks.

### [90] [Predicting Invoice Dilution in Supply Chain Finance with Leakage Free Two Stage XGBoost, KAN (Kolmogorov Arnold Networks), and Ensemble Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15248)
*Pavel Koptev,Vishnu Kumar,Konstantin Malkov,George Shapiro,Yury Vikhanov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Invoice or payment dilution is the gap between the approved invoice amount and the actual collection is a significant source of non credit risk and margin loss in supply chain finance. Traditionally, this risk is managed through the buyer's irrevocable payment undertaking (IPU), which commits to full payment without deductions. However, IPUs can hinder supply chain finance adoption, particularly among sub-invested grade buyers. A newer, data-driven methods use real-time dynamic credit limits, projecting dilution for each buyer-supplier pair in real-time. This paper introduces an AI, machine learning framework and evaluates how that can supplement a deterministic algorithm to predict invoice dilution using extensive production dataset across nine key transaction fields.

Traditionally, this risk is managed through the buyer's irrevocable payment undertaking (IPU), which commits to full payment without deductions. ### [91] [Enhancing Diversity and Feasibility: Joint Population Synthesis from Multi-source Data Using Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15270)
*Farbod Abbasi,Zachary Patterson,Bilal Farooq*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Generating realistic synthetic populations is essential for agent-based models (ABM) in transportation and urban planning. Current methods face two major limitations. First, many rely on a single dataset or follow a sequential data fusion and generation process, which means they fail to capture the complex interplay between features. Second, these approaches struggle with sampling zeros (valid but unobserved attribute combinations) and structural zeros (infeasible combinations due to logical constraints), which reduce the diversity and feasibility of the generated data. This study proposes a novel method to simultaneously integrate and synthesize multi-source datasets using a Wasserstein Generative Adversarial Network (WGAN) with gradient penalty. This joint learning method improves both the diversity and feasibility of synthetic data by defining a regularization term (inverse gradient penalty) for the generator loss function. For the evaluation, we implement a unified evaluation metric for similarity, and place special emphasis on measuring diversity and feasibility through recall, precision, and the F1 score. Results show that the proposed joint approach outperforms the sequential baseline, with recall increasing by 7\% and precision by 15\%. Additionally, the regularization term further improves diversity and feasibility, reflected in a 10\% increase in recall and 1\% in precision. We assess similarity distributions using a five-metric score. The joint approach performs better overall, and reaches a score of 88.1 compared to 84.6 for the sequential method. Since synthetic populations serve as a key input for ABM, this multi-source generative approach has the potential to significantly enhance the accuracy and reliability of ABM.

Conclusion: AI enhancement unavailable (ai_circuit_open). Original arXiv metadata is preserved.

Since synthetic populations serve as a key input for ABM, this multi-source generative approach has the potential to significantly enhance the accuracy and reliability of ABM.

</details>


### [92] [When Remembering and Planning are Worth it: Navigating under Change](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15274)
*Omid Madani,J. Brian Burns,Reza Eghbali,Thomas L. Dean*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: We explore how different types and uses of memory can aid spatial navigation in changing uncertain environments. In the simple foraging task we study, every day, our agent has to find its way from its home, through barriers, to food. Moreover, the world is non-stationary: from day to day, the location of the barriers and food may change, and the agent's sensing such as its location information is uncertain and very limited. Any model construction, such as a map, and use, such as planning, needs to be robust against these challenges, and if any learning is to be useful, it needs to be adequately fast. We look at a range of strategies, from simple to sophisticated, with various uses of memory and learning. We find that an architecture that can incorporate multiple strategies is required to handle (sub)tasks of a different nature, in particular for exploration and search, when food location is not known, and for planning a good path to a remembered (likely) food location. An agent that utilizes non-stationary probability learning techniques to keep updating its (episodic) memories and that uses those memories to build maps and plan on the fly (imperfect maps, i.e. noisy and limited to the agent's experience) can be increasingly and substantially more efficient than the simpler (minimal-memory) agents, as the task difficulties such as distance to goal are raised, as long as the uncertainty, from localization and change, is not too large.

### [93] [EAA: Automating materials characterization with vision language model agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15294)
*Ming Du,Yanqi Luo,Srutarshi Banerjee,Michael Wojcik,Jelena Popovic,Mathew J. Cherukara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: We present Experiment Automation Agents (EAA), a vision-language-model-driven agentic system designed to automate complex experimental microscopy workflows. EAA integrates multimodal reasoning, tool-augmented action, and optional long-term memory to support both autonomous procedures and interactive user-guided measurements. Built on a flexible task-manager architecture, the system enables workflows ranging from fully agent-driven automation to logic-defined routines that embed localized LLM queries. EAA further provides a modern tool ecosystem with two-way compatibility for Model Context Protocol (MCP), allowing instrument-control tools to be consumed or served across applications. We demonstrate EAA at an imaging beamline at the Advanced Photon Source, including automated zone plate focusing, natural language-described feature search, and interactive data acquisition. These results illustrate how vision-capable agents can enhance beamline efficiency, reduce operational burden, and lower the expertise barrier for users.

### [94] [X-MAP: eXplainable Misclassification Analysis and Profiling for Spam and Phishing Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15298)
*Qi Zhang,Dian Chen,Lance M. Kaplan,Audun Jøsang,Dong Hyun Jeong,Feng Chen,Jin-Hee Cho*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Misclassifications in spam and phishing detection are very harmful, as false negatives expose users to attacks while false positives degrade trust. Existing uncertainty-based detectors can flag potential errors, but possibly be deceived and offer limited interpretability. This paper presents X-MAP, an eXplainable Misclassification Analysis and Profilling framework that reveals topic-level semantic patterns behind model failures. X-MAP combines SHAP-based feature attributions with non-negative matrix factorization to build interpretable topic profiles for reliably classified spam/phishing and legitimate messages, and measures each message's deviation from these profiles using Jensen-Shannon divergence. Experiments on SMS and phishing datasets show that misclassified messages exhibit at least two times larger divergence than correctly classified ones. As a detector, X-MAP achieves up to 0.98 AUROC and lowers the false-rejection rate at 95% TRR to 0.089 on positive predictions. When used as a repair layer on base detectors, it recovers up to 97% of falsely rejected correct predictions with moderate leakage. These results demonstrate X-MAP's effectiveness and interpretability for improving spam and phishing detection.

### [95] [AgriWorld:A World Tools Protocol Framework for Verifiable Agricultural Reasoning with Code-Executing LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15325)
*Zhixing Zhang,Jesen Zhang,Hao Liu,Qinhan Lv,Jing Yang,Kaitong Cai,Keze Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Foundation models for agriculture are increasingly trained on massive spatiotemporal data (e.g., multi-spectral remote sensing, soil grids, and field-level management logs) and achieve strong performance on forecasting and monitoring. However, these models lack language-based reasoning and interactive capabilities, limiting their usefulness in real-world agronomic workflows. Meanwhile, large language models (LLMs) excel at interpreting and generating text, but cannot directly reason over high-dimensional, heterogeneous agricultural datasets. We bridge this gap with an agentic framework for agricultural science. It provides a Python execution environment, AgriWorld, exposing unified tools for geospatial queries over field parcels, remote-sensing time-series analytics, crop growth simulation, and task-specific predictors (e.g., yield, stress, and disease risk). On top of this environment, we design a multi-turn LLM agent, Agro-Reflective, that iteratively writes code, observes execution results, and refines its analysis via an execute-observe-refine loop. We introduce AgroBench, with scalable data generation for diverse agricultural QA spanning lookups, forecasting, anomaly detection, and counterfactual "what-if" analysis. Experiments outperform text-only and direct tool-use baselines, validating execution-driven reflection for reliable agricultural reasoning.

### [96] [World-Model-Augmented Web Agents with Action Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15384)
*Zhouzhou Shen,Xueyu Hu,Xiyun Li,Tianqing Fang,Juncheng Li,Shengyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Web agents based on large language models have demonstrated promising capability in automating web tasks. However, current web agents struggle to reason out sensible actions due to the limitations of predicting environment changes, and might not possess comprehensive awareness of execution risks, prematurely performing risky actions that cause losses and lead to task failure. To address these challenges, we propose WAC, a web agent that integrates model collaboration, consequence simulation, and feedback-driven action refinement. To overcome the cognitive isolation of individual models, we introduce a multi-agent collaboration process that enables an action model to consult a world model as a web-environment expert for strategic guidance; the action model then grounds these suggestions into executable actions, leveraging prior knowledge of environmental state transition dynamics to enhance candidate action proposal. To achieve risk-aware resilient task execution, we introduce a two-stage deduction chain. A world model, specialized in environmental state transitions, simulates action outcomes, which a judge model then scrutinizes to trigger action corrective feedback when necessary. Experiments show that WAC achieves absolute gains of 1.8% on VisualWebArena and 1.3% on Online-Mind2Web.

### [97] [Improving LLM Reliability through Hybrid Abstention and Adaptive Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15391)
*Ankit Sharma,Nachiket Tapas,Jyotiprakash Patra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于上下文感知的自适应弃权系统，通过动态调整安全阈值和级联检测架构，在保证LLM安全性的同时减少误报并提升响应速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在生产环境部署时面临安全性与实用性的根本性权衡：严格的过滤机制会误伤良性查询，宽松控制则存在安全隐患。传统基于静态规则或固定阈值的防护机制缺乏上下文敏感性，计算成本高、延迟大，导致用户体验下降。

Method: 提出自适应弃权系统，基于实时上下文信号（领域、用户历史等）动态调整安全阈值。构建包含五个并行检测器的多维检测架构，采用分层级联机制组合，通过逐步过滤查询优化速度和精度，减少不必要计算。

Result: 级联设计相比非级联模型和外部防护系统显著降低延迟。在混合和特定领域工作负载评估中，假阳性显著减少，尤其在医疗建议和创意写作等敏感领域。在严格操作模式下保持高安全精度和近乎完美的召回率。

Conclusion: 该上下文感知的弃权框架有效平衡了安全性和实用性，同时保持了系统性能，为可靠的LLM部署提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) deployed in production environments face a fundamental safety-utility trade-off either a strict filtering mechanisms prevent harmful outputs but often block benign queries or a relaxed controls risk unsafe content generation. Conventional guardrails based on static rules or fixed confidence thresholds are typically context-insensitive and computationally expensive, resulting in high latency and degraded user experience. To address these limitations, we introduce an adaptive abstention system that dynamically adjusts safety thresholds based on real-time contextual signals such as domain and user history. The proposed framework integrates a multi-dimensional detection architecture composed of five parallel detectors, combined through a hierarchical cascade mechanism to optimize both speed and precision. The cascade design reduces unnecessary computation by progressively filtering queries, achieving substantial latency improvements compared to non-cascaded models and external guardrail systems. Extensive evaluation on mixed and domain-specific workloads demonstrates significant reductions in false positives, particularly in sensitive domains such as medical advice and creative writing. The system maintains high safety precision and near-perfect recall under strict operating modes. Overall, our context-aware abstention framework effectively balances safety and utility while preserving performance, offering a scalable solution for reliable LLM deployment.

</details>


### [98] [Common Belief Revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15403)
*Thomas Ågotnes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Contrary to common belief, common belief is not KD4.
  If individual belief is KD45, common belief does indeed lose the 5 property and keep the D and 4 properties -- and it has none of the other commonly considered properties of knowledge and belief. But it has another property: $C(Cφ\rightarrow φ)$ -- corresponding to so-called shift-reflexivity (reflexivity one step ahead). This observation begs the question:
  is KD4 extended with this axiom a complete characterisation of common belief in the KD45 case? If not, what \emph{is} the logic of common belief? In this paper we show that the answer to the first question is ``no'': there is one additional axiom, and, furthermore, it relies on the number of agents. We show that the result is a complete characterisation of common belief, settling the open problem.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: AI enhancement unavailable; see the original abstract.

Conclusion: AI enhancement unavailable (ai_circuit_open). Original arXiv metadata is preserved.

### [99] [Quantifying construct validity in large language model evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15532)
*Ryan Othniel Kearns*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: The LLM community often reports benchmark results as if they are synonymous with general model capabilities. However, benchmarks can have problems that distort performance, like test set contamination and annotator error. How can we know that a benchmark is a reliable indicator of some capability that we want to measure? This question concerns the construct validity of LLM benchmarks, and it requires separating benchmark results from capabilities when we model and predict LLM performance.
  Both social scientists and computer scientists propose formal models - latent factor models and scaling laws - for identifying the capabilities underlying benchmark scores. However, neither technique is satisfactory for construct validity. Latent factor models ignore scaling laws, and as a result, the capabilities they extract often proxy model size. Scaling laws ignore measurement error, and as a result, the capabilities they extract are both uninterpretable and overfit to the observed benchmarks.
  This thesis presents the structured capabilities model, the first model to extract interpretable and generalisable capabilities from a large collection of LLM benchmark results. I fit this model and its two alternatives on a large sample of results from the OpenLLM Leaderboard. Structured capabilities outperform latent factor models on parsimonious fit indices, and exhibit better out-of-distribution benchmark prediction than scaling laws. These improvements are possible because neither existing approach separates model scale from capabilities in the appropriate way. Model scale should inform capabilities, as in scaling laws, and these capabilities should inform observed results up to measurement error, as in latent factor models. In combining these two insights, structured capabilities demonstrate better explanatory and predictive power for quantifying construct validity in LLM evaluations.

### [100] [RUVA: Personalized Transparent On-Device Graph Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15553)
*Gabriele Conte,Alessio Mattiace,Gianni Carmosino,Potito Aghilar,Giovanni Servedio,Francesco Musicco,Vito Walter Anelli,Tommaso Di Noia,Francesco Maria Donini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Ruva提出了首个"玻璃盒"个人AI架构，通过个人知识图取代传统向量数据库，实现人机协作记忆管理，确保用户可审查AI知识并精确删除特定事实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前个人AI主导的"黑盒"检索增强生成存在根本性问题：标准向量数据库虽然提供统计匹配，但缺乏可解释性。当AI产生幻觉或检索敏感数据时，用户无法检查原因或纠正错误。更严重的是，从向量空间中"删除"概念在数学上不精确，会留下违反真正隐私的概率性"幽灵"数据。

Method: 提出Ruva，首个面向人机协作记忆管理的"玻璃盒"架构。Ruva将个人AI基于个人知识图构建，使用户能够审查AI所知内容并对特定事实进行精确删除。通过将范式从向量匹配转向图推理，确保"被遗忘权"的实现。

Result: Ruva架构确保了用户的"被遗忘权"，使用户成为自己生活的编辑者。系统提供了透明化的个人AI记忆管理能力，用户可以精确控制AI记忆中的信息。项目及演示视频已公开可用。

Conclusion: Ruva通过知识图推理替代向量匹配，将控制权交给用户，为个人AI提供了可解释、可控的记忆管理方案，解决了现有黑盒架构中的隐私和可审计性问题。

Abstract: The Personal AI landscape is currently dominated by "Black Box" Retrieval-Augmented Generation. While standard vector databases offer statistical matching, they suffer from a fundamental lack of accountability: when an AI hallucinates or retrieves sensitive data, the user cannot inspect the cause nor correct the error. Worse, "deleting" a concept from a vector space is mathematically imprecise, leaving behind probabilistic "ghosts" that violate true privacy. We propose Ruva, the first "Glass Box" architecture designed for Human-in-the-Loop Memory Curation. Ruva grounds Personal AI in a Personal Knowledge Graph, enabling users to inspect what the AI knows and to perform precise redaction of specific facts. By shifting the paradigm from Vector Matching to Graph Reasoning, Ruva ensures the "Right to be Forgotten." Users are the editors of their own lives; Ruva hands them the pen. The project and the demo video are available at http://sisinf00.poliba.it/ruva/.

</details>


### [101] [How Vision Becomes Language: A Layer-wise Information-Theoretic Analysis of Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15580)
*Hongxuan Wu,Yukun Zhang,Xueqing Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: When a multimodal Transformer answers a visual question, is the prediction driven by visual evidence, linguistic reasoning, or genuinely fused cross-modal computation -- and how does this structure evolve across layers? We address this question with a layer-wise framework based on Partial Information Decomposition (PID) that decomposes the predictive information at each Transformer layer into redundant, vision-unique, language-unique, and synergistic components. To make PID tractable for high-dimensional neural representations, we introduce \emph{PID Flow}, a pipeline combining dimensionality reduction, normalizing-flow Gaussianization, and closed-form Gaussian PID estimation. Applying this framework to LLaVA-1.5-7B and LLaVA-1.6-7B across six GQA reasoning tasks, we uncover a consistent \emph{modal transduction} pattern: visual-unique information peaks early and decays with depth, language-unique information surges in late layers to account for roughly 82\% of the final prediction, and cross-modal synergy remains below 2\%. This trajectory is highly stable across model variants (layer-wise correlations $>$0.96) yet strongly task-dependent, with semantic redundancy governing the detailed information fingerprint. To establish causality, we perform targeted Image$\rightarrow$Question attention knockouts and show that disrupting the primary transduction pathway induces predictable increases in trapped visual-unique information, compensatory synergy, and total information cost -- effects that are strongest in vision-dependent tasks and weakest in high-redundancy tasks. Together, these results provide an information-theoretic, causal account of how vision becomes language in multimodal Transformers, and offer quantitative guidance for identifying architectural bottlenecks where modality-specific information is lost.

### [102] [On inferring cumulative constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15635)
*Konstantin Sidorov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Cumulative constraints are central in scheduling with constraint programming, yet propagation is typically performed per constraint, missing multi-resource interactions and causing severe slowdowns on some benchmarks. I present a preprocessing method for inferring additional cumulative constraints that capture such interactions without search-time probing. This approach interprets cumulative constraints as linear inequalities over occupancy vectors and generates valid inequalities by (i) discovering covers, the sets of tasks that cannot run in parallel, (ii) strengthening the cover inequalities for the discovered sets with lifting, and (iii) injecting the resulting constraints back into the scheduling problem instance. Experiments on standard RCPSP and RCPSP/max test suites show that these inferred constraints improve search performance and tighten objective bounds on favorable instances, while incurring little degradation on unfavorable ones. Additionally, these experiments discover 25 new lower bounds and five new best solutions; eight of the lower bounds are obtained directly from the inferred constraints.

This approach interprets cumulative constraints as linear inequalities over occupancy vectors and generates valid inequalities by (i) discovering covers, the sets of tasks that cannot run in parallel, (ii) strengthening the cover inequalities for the discovered sets with lifting, and (iii) injecting the resulting constraints back into the scheduling problem instance. Experiments on standard RCPSP and RCPSP/max test suites show that these inferred constraints improve search performance and tighten objective bounds on favorable instances, while incurring little degradation on unfavorable ones. Additionally, these experiments discover 25 new lower bounds and five new best solutions; eight of the lower bounds are obtained directly from the inferred constraints.

</details>


### [103] [CARE Drive A Framework for Evaluating Reason-Responsiveness of Vision Language Models in Automated Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15645)
*Lucas Elbert Suryana,Farah Bierenga,Sanne van Buuren,Pepijn Kooij,Elsefien Tulleners,Federico Scari,Simeon Calvert,Bart van Arem,Arkady Zgonnikov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了CARE Drive（Context Aware Reasons Evaluation for Driving），一个用于评估自动驾驶中视觉语言模型是否真正响应人类推理而非仅作事后合理化的模型无关评估框架。



<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶评估方法主要关注基于结果的安全性和轨迹准确性等性能指标，但无法判断模型决策是否真正反映了人类相关的考量因素。这种局限性导致难以区分模型解释是源于真正的推理响应决策还是仅做事后合理化，在安全关键领域尤其危险，可能产生虚假的信心。


Method: CARE Drive采用两阶段评估流程：1）提示校准确保稳定输出；2）系统化的上下文扰动测量模型对人类理由（安全边际、社会压力、效率约束等）的决策敏感性。框架通过受控的上下文变化对比基线和增强推理的模型决策，评估人类理由是否因果性地影响决策行为。


Result: 在涉及竞争性规范考量的超车场景中演示表明，明确的人类理由显著影响模型决策，提升了与专家推荐行为的一致性。但响应性在不同上下文因素间存在差异，显示模型对不同类型理由的敏感度不均匀。


Conclusion: 研究结果为无需修改模型参数即可系统性评估基础模型的推理响应性提供了实证证据，有助于在安全关键应用中区分真正的推理驱动决策与事后合理化。

Abstract: Foundation models, including vision language models, are increasingly used in automated driving to interpret scenes, recommend actions, and generate natural language explanations. However, existing evaluation methods primarily assess outcome based performance, such as safety and trajectory accuracy, without determining whether model decisions reflect human relevant considerations. As a result, it remains unclear whether explanations produced by such models correspond to genuine reason responsive decision making or merely post hoc rationalizations. This limitation is especially significant in safety critical domains because it can create false confidence. To address this gap, we propose CARE Drive, Context Aware Reasons Evaluation for Driving, a model agnostic framework for evaluating reason responsiveness in vision language models applied to automated driving. CARE Drive compares baseline and reason augmented model decisions under controlled contextual variation to assess whether human reasons causally influence decision behavior. The framework employs a two stage evaluation process. Prompt calibration ensures stable outputs. Systematic contextual perturbation then measures decision sensitivity to human reasons such as safety margins, social pressure, and efficiency constraints. We demonstrate CARE Drive in a cyclist overtaking scenario involving competing normative considerations. Results show that explicit human reasons significantly influence model decisions, improving alignment with expert recommended behavior. However, responsiveness varies across contextual factors, indicating uneven sensitivity to different types of reasons. These findings provide empirical evidence that reason responsiveness in foundation models can be systematically evaluated without modifying model parameters.

</details>


### [104] [PERSONA: Dynamic and Compositional Inference-Time Personality Control via Activation Vector Algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15669)
*Xiachong Feng,Liang Zhao,Weihong Zhong,Yichong Huang,Yuxuan Gu,Lingpeng Kong,Xiaocheng Feng,Bing Qin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了PERSONA，一个无需训练的大型语言模型个性控制框架，通过在激活空间中直接操作个性向量，实现了微调级别的性能表现。该方法利用个性特征在模型表示空间中近似正交且可代数运算的特性，通过三个阶段实现精确控制。实验结果显示，在PersonalityBench上达到9.60分（接近微调上限9.61），在动态个性适应基准上达到91%胜率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM个性控制方法主要依赖静态提示或昂贵的微调，这些方法存在两个关键缺陷：一是无法捕捉人类特征的动态性，二是无法实现特征的组合性。因此需要一种无需训练且能有效控制个性特征的方法。

Method: PERSONA框架基于关键洞察：个性特征在模型表示空间中表现为可提取的近似正交方向，支持代数运算。框架包含三个阶段：1) Persona-Base通过对比激活分析提取正交特征向量；2) Persona-Algebra通过向量运算实现精确控制（标量乘法控制强度，加法组合特征，减法抑制特征）；3) Persona-Flow在推理过程中动态组合向量以实现上下文感知适应。

Result: 在PersonalityBench评估中，PERSONA平均得分9.60，几乎达到监督微调上限9.61（无需任何梯度更新）；在新的Persona-Evolve动态个性适应基准测试中，在多个不同模型系列中最高达到91%的胜率。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，大型语言模型个性的某些方面在数学上是可处理的，这为可解释且高效的行为控制开辟了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Current methods for personality control in Large Language Models rely on static prompting or expensive fine-tuning, failing to capture the dynamic and compositional nature of human traits. We introduce PERSONA, a training-free framework that achieves fine-tuning level performance through direct manipulation of personality vectors in activation space. Our key insight is that personality traits appear as extractable, approximately orthogonal directions in the model's representation space that support algebraic operations. The framework operates through three stages: Persona-Base extracts orthogonal trait vectors via contrastive activation analysis; Persona-Algebra enables precise control through vector arithmetic (scalar multiplication for intensity, addition for composition, subtraction for suppression); and Persona-Flow achieves context-aware adaptation by dynamically composing these vectors during inference. On PersonalityBench, our approach achieves a mean score of 9.60, nearly matching the supervised fine-tuning upper bound of 9.61 without any gradient updates. On our proposed Persona-Evolve benchmark for dynamic personality adaptation, we achieve up to 91% win rates across diverse model families. These results provide evidence that aspects of LLM personality are mathematically tractable, opening new directions for interpretable and efficient behavioral control.

</details>


### [105] [Recursive Concept Evolution for Compositional Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15725)
*Sarim Chaudhry*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Large language models achieve strong performance on many complex reasoning tasks, yet their accuracy degrades sharply on benchmarks that require compositional reasoning, including ARC-AGI-2, GPQA, MATH, BBH, and HLE. Existing methods improve reasoning by expanding token-level search through chain-of-thought prompting, self-consistency, or reinforcement learning, but they leave the model's latent representation space fixed. When the required abstraction is not already encoded in this space, performance collapses. We propose Recursive Concept Evolution (RCE), a framework that enables pretrained language models to modify their internal representation geometry during inference. RCE introduces dynamically generated low-rank concept subspaces that are spawned when representational inadequacy is detected, selected through a minimum description length criterion, merged when synergistic, and consolidated via constrained optimization to preserve stability. This process allows the model to construct new abstractions rather than recombining existing ones. We integrate RCE with Mistral-7B and evaluate it across compositional reasoning benchmarks. RCE yields 12-18 point gains on ARC-AGI-2, 8-14 point improvements on GPQA and BBH, and consistent reductions in depth-induced error on MATH and HLE.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI enhancement unavailable (ai_circuit_open). Original arXiv metadata is preserved.
